     2	import { Stack, usePathname } from "expo-router";

     4	import { StripeProvider } from "@stripe/stripe-react-native";
     5	import * as NavigationBar from "expo-navigation-bar";

     7	  Animated,
     8	  AppState,
     9	  Platform,
    10	  Text,
    11	  TextInput,

    16	  useSafeAreaInsets,
    17	} from "react-native-safe-area-context";
    18	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

    20	import AppHeader from "../components/AppHeader";
    21	import MainScreenPushFrame from "../components/MainScreenPushFrame";
    22	import ScreenFade from "../components/ScreenFade";

    24	import {
    25	  BackgroundHeroStateProvider,
    26	  useBackgroundHeroState,

    41	const navigationTheme = {
    42	  ...DefaultTheme,
    43	  colors: {
    44	    ...DefaultTheme.colors,
    45	    background:
    46	      Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.background,
    47	    card: Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.card,
    48	  },
    49	};
    50	const androidNavigationBarColor = "#f7b967";
    51	const androidNavigationBarHairlineColor = "rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.28)";
    52	const androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth = 0.375;
    53	const androidNavigationBarButtonStyle = "light";
    54	
    55	function disableAutomaticFontScaling(Component) {
    56	  Component.defaultProps = Component.defaultProps || {};
    57	  Component.defaultProps.allowFontScaling = false;
    58	  Component.defaultProps.maxFontSizeMultiplier = 1;
    59	}
    60	
    61	disableAutomaticFontScaling(Text);
    62	disableAutomaticFontScaling(TextInput);
    63	
    64	async function applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme() {
    65	  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
    66	
    67	  try {
    68	    await NavigationBar.setVisibilityAsync("visible");
    69	  } catch {}
    70	
    71	  try {
    72	    await NavigationBar.setButtonStyleAsync(androidNavigationBarButtonStyle);
    73	  } catch {}
    74	}
    75	
    76	function AndroidNavigationBarTint({ pathname }) {
    77	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    78	
    79	  useEffect(() => {
    80	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") return undefined;
    81	
    82	    applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme();
    83	    const restoreTimer = setTimeout(applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme, 250);
    84	    const finalRestoreTimer = setTimeout(applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme, 1000);
    85	    const appStateSubscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
    86	      if (state === "active") {
    87	        applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme();
    88	      }
    89	    });
    90	    let visibilitySubscription;
    91	
    92	    try {
    93	      visibilitySubscription = NavigationBar.addVisibilityListener(
    94	        applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme
    95	      );
    96	    } catch {}
    97	
    98	    return () => {
    99	      clearTimeout(restoreTimer);
   100	      clearTimeout(finalRestoreTimer);
   101	      appStateSubscription.remove();
   102	      visibilitySubscription?.remove();
   103	    };
   104	  }, [pathname]);
   105	
   106	  if (Platform.OS !== "android" || insets.bottom <= 0) {
   107	    return null;
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  return (
   111	    <View
   112	      pointerEvents="none"
   113	      style={{
   114	        position: "absolute",
   115	        left: 0,
   116	        right: 0,
   117	        bottom: 0,
   118	        height: insets.bottom,
   119	        borderTopWidth: androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth,
   120	        borderTopColor: androidNavigationBarHairlineColor,
   121	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   122	        zIndex: 1000001,
   123	        elevation: 1000001,
   124	      }}
   125	    />
   126	  );
   127	}
   128	
   129	function RootLayoutContent({ headerScrollY }) {
   130	  const pathname = usePathname();
   131	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
   132	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   133	  const { cartOverlayActionRequest, isShopOverlayVisible } = useShopCart();
   134	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   135	  const topSafeInset = getTopSafeInset(insets);
   136	
   137	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
   138	
   139	  const useOverlayHeader =
   140	    pathname === "/" ||
   141	    pathname === "/products" ||
   142	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
   143	    pathname === "/contact";
   144	
   145	  const shouldProtectBackgroundHero =
   146	    useOverlayHeader &&
   147	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive || backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen);
   148	  const backgroundHeroLayerDepth = shouldProtectBackgroundHero ? 100 : 0;
   149	  const screenFadeTopOffset = pathname === "/shop"
   150	    ? 120 + topSafeInset
   151	    : 84 + topSafeInset;
   152	  const shouldShowScreenFade =
   153	    !(pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible);
   154	  const shouldShowCartOpeningDim =
   155	    cartOverlayActionRequest.pending && pathname !== "/shop";
   156	
   157	  return (
   158	    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
   159	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   160	        <View
   161	          pointerEvents="none"
   162	          style={{
   163	            position: "absolute",
   164	            top: 0,
   165	            left: 0,
   166	            right: 0,
   167	            zIndex: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   168	            elevation: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   169	          }}
   170	        >
   171	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showOnlyHero />
   172	        </View>
   173	      ) : null}
   174	
   175	      <MainScreenPushFrame>
   176	        <Stack
   177	          screenOptions={{
   178	            headerShown: false,
   179	            animation: "none",
   180	            contentStyle: {
   181	              backgroundColor: useOverlayHeader
   182	                ? "transparent"
   183	                : "#FFFCF2",
   184	            },
   185	          }}
   186	        />
   187	      </MainScreenPushFrame>
   188	
   189	      {shouldShowScreenFade ? (
   190	        <ScreenFade
   191	          showTopFade={!useOverlayHeader}
   192	          topOffset={screenFadeTopOffset}
   193	        />
   194	      ) : null}
   195	
   196	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   197	        <View
   198	          pointerEvents="box-none"
   199	          style={{
   200	            position: "absolute",
   201	            top: 0,
   202	            left: 0,
   203	            right: 0,
   204	            zIndex: 1000000,
   205	            elevation: 1000000,
   206	          }}
   207	        >
   208	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showHero={false} />
   209	        </View>
   210	      ) : null}
   211	
   212	      {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
   213	        <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
   214	      ) : null}
   215	
   216	      {shouldShowCartOpeningDim ? (
   217	        <View
   218	          pointerEvents="none"
   219	          style={{
   220	            position: "absolute",
   221	            top: 0,
   222	            right: 0,
   223	            bottom: 0,
   224	            left: 0,
   225	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   226	            zIndex: 1000001,
   227	            elevation: 1000001,
   228	          }}
   229	        />
   230	      ) : null}
   231	
   232	      <StickyCartButton />
   233	
   234	      <AndroidNavigationBarTint pathname={pathname} />
   235	    </View>
   236	  );
   237	}
   238	
   239	export default function RootLayout() {
   240	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   241	
   242	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
   243	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
   244	    "Great Vibes": require("../assets/fonts/great_vibes/GreatVibes-Regular.ttf"),
   245	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
   246	    "TT Fors Demibold": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_demibold.ttf"),
   247	    "TT Fors Light": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_light.ttf"),
   248	  });
   249	
   250	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
   251	    return null;
   252	  }
   253	
   254	  return (
   255	    <SafeAreaProvider>
   256	      <StripeProvider
   257	        merchantIdentifier={stripeMerchantIdentifier || undefined}
   258	        publishableKey={stripePublishableKey}
   259	        setReturnUrlSchemeOnAndroid
   260	        urlScheme={stripeUrlScheme}
   261	      >
   262	        <ThemeProvider value={navigationTheme}>
   263	          <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
   264	            <BackgroundHeroStateProvider sourceScrollY={headerScrollY}>
   265	              <HeaderSwipeProvider>
   266	                <ShopCartProvider>
   267	                  <RootLayoutContent headerScrollY={headerScrollY} />
   268	                </ShopCartProvider>
   269	              </HeaderSwipeProvider>
   270	            </BackgroundHeroStateProvider>
   271	          </HeaderScrollProvider>
   272	        </ThemeProvider>
   273	      </StripeProvider>
   274	    </SafeAreaProvider>
   275	  );
   276	}

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import {
     3	  Animated,
     4	  Image,
     5	  StyleSheet,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	  useWindowDimensions,
     9	} from "react-native";
    10	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
    11	
    12	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
    13	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
    14	import PageDivider from "../components/PageDivider";
    15	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
    16	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    17	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    18	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    19	
    20	const passionImageBlendScrollDistance = 720;
    21	const passionImageBlendFeatherHeight = 170;
    22	const passionImageBlendOverlayColor = "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.92)";
    23	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    24	const homeFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    25	const homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    26	
    27	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    28	  return (
    29	    currentLayout &&
    30	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    31	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    32	  );
    33	}
    34	
    35	export default function HomeScreen() {
    36	  const [imageLayouts, setImageLayouts] = useState({});
    37	  const [paragraphLayouts, setParagraphLayouts] = useState({});
    38	  const {
    39	    compactTopLayout,
    40	    initialContentOffset,
    41	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    42	    scrollHandlers,
    43	    scrollY,
    44	  } =
    45	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    46	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    47	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    48	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
    49	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    50	  const passionImageBlendTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    51	    inputRange: [0, passionImageBlendScrollDistance],
    52	    outputRange: [-passionImageBlendFeatherHeight, croppedImageHeight],
    53	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    54	  });
    55	  const handleParagraphLayout = useCallback(
    56	    (featureName) =>
    57	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    58	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    59	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    60	
    61	        setParagraphLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    62	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    63	            return currentLayouts;
    64	          }
    65	
    66	          return {
    67	            ...currentLayouts,
    68	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    69	          };
    70	        });
    71	      },
    72	    []
    73	  );
    74	  const handleImageLayout = useCallback(
    75	    (featureName) =>
    76	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    77	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    78	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    79	
    80	        setImageLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    81	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    82	            return currentLayouts;
    83	          }
    84	
    85	          return {
    86	            ...currentLayouts,
    87	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    88	          };
    89	        });
    90	      },
    91	    []
    92	  );
    93	
    94	  return (
    95	    <View style={sharedStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    96	      <Animated.ScrollView
    97	        style={sharedStyles.scroll}
    98	        contentContainerStyle={[
    99	          sharedStyles.scrollContent,
   100	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   101	        ]}
   102	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   103	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   104	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   105	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   106	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   107	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   108	        {...scrollHandlers}
   109	      >
   110	        <View style={sharedStyles.main}>
   111	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   112	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   113	            pageTitleStyle={[sharedStyles.pageTitle, homeStyles.pageTitle]}
   114	            scrollY={scrollY}
   115	          />
   116	
   117	          <CenterMagnifyView
   118	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.passion}
   119	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.passion}
   120	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   121	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   122	            scrollY={scrollY}
   123	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   124	          >
   125	            <View
   126	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("passion")}
   127	              style={[
   128	                sharedStyles.directionalImageBlendWrap,
   129	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
   130	              ]}
   131	            >
   132	              <Image
   133	                pointerEvents="none"
   134	                source={require("../passion111_mos9_bright_italian_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   135	                style={[
   136	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   137	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   138	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   139	                ]}
   140	                resizeMode="contain"
   141	              />
   142	              <Animated.View
   143	                pointerEvents="none"
   144	                style={[
   145	                  sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendPanel,
   146	                  {
   147	                    height:
   148	                      croppedImageHeight + passionImageBlendFeatherHeight,
   149	                    transform: [{ translateY: passionImageBlendTranslateY }],
   150	                  },
   151	                ]}
   152	              >
   153	                <LinearGradient
   154	                  colors={[
   155	                    "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
   156	                    passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   157	                  ]}
   158	                  locations={[0, 1]}
   159	                  style={sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendFeather}
   160	                />
   161	                <View
   162	                  style={[
   163	                    sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendSolid,
   164	                    {
   165	                      height: croppedImageHeight,
   166	                      backgroundColor: passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   167	                    },
   168	                  ]}
   169	                />
   170	              </Animated.View>
   171	            </View>
   172	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   173	              Passion
   174	            </Text>
   175	            <Text
   176	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("passion")}
   177	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   178	            >
   179	              Every Alla Vostra board is prepared with care, precision, and a

   181	            </Text>

    95	const orangeButtonGradientColors = ["#FFC878", "#f7b967", "#D9953F"];
    96	
    97	function ProductOrangeButtonGradient() {
    98	  return (
    99	    <LinearGradient
   100	      colors={orangeButtonGradientColors}
   101	      locations={[0, 0.52, 1]}
   102	      pointerEvents="none"
   103	      start={{ x: 0.5, y: 0 }}
   104	      end={{ x: 0.5, y: 1 }}
   105	      style={productsStyles.buyButtonGradient}
   106	    />
   107	  );
   108	}
   109	
   110	function ProductSection({
   111	  croppedImageWidth,
   112	  onBuyPress,
   113	  onBuyButtonLayout,
   114	  onImageLayout,
   115	  onLowerContentLayout,
   116	  product,
   117	}) {
   118	  return (
   119	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
   120	      <View onLayout={onImageLayout} style={{ width: croppedImageWidth }}>
   121	        <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>
   122	          <Image
   123	            pointerEvents="none"
   124	            source={product.image}
   125	            style={[
   126	              productsStyles.productImage,
   127	              { height: croppedImageWidth },
   128	            ]}
   129	            resizeMode="contain"
   130	            accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
   131	          />
   132	        </View>
   133	      </View>
   134	
   135	      <View
   136	        onLayout={onLowerContentLayout}
   137	        style={productsStyles.productLowerContent}
   138	      >
   139	        <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   140	        <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   141	
   142	        <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   143	          {product.includes.map((section) => (
   144	            <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   145	              <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   146	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   147	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   148	              </View>
   149	
   150	              {section.items.map((item) => (
   151	                <View key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItemRow}>
   152	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleBorder}>
   153	                    <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleOuter} />
   154	                    <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangle} />
   155	                  </View>
   156	                  <Text style={productsStyles.includeItem}>{item}</Text>
   157	                </View>
   158	              ))}
   159	
   160	              {section.note ? (
   161	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   162	              ) : null}
   163	            </View>
   164	          ))}
   165	        </View>
   166	
   167	        <Pressable
   168	          accessibilityLabel={`Buy ${product.title}`}
   169	          accessibilityRole="button"
   170	          onLayout={onBuyButtonLayout}
   171	          onPress={onBuyPress}
   172	          style={({ pressed }) => [
   173	            productsStyles.buyButton,
   174	            pressed && productsStyles.buyButtonPressed,
   175	          ]}
   176	        >
   177	          <ProductOrangeButtonGradient />
   178	          <Text
   179	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
   180	            allowFontScaling={false}
   181	            minimumFontScale={0.72}
   182	            numberOfLines={1}
   183	            style={productsStyles.buyButtonText}
   184	          >
   185	            {`Buy ${product.title}`}
   186	          </Text>
   187	        </Pressable>
   188	      </View>
   189	    </View>
   190	  );
   191	}
   192	
   193	function ProductMagnifySection({ croppedImageWidth, onBuyPress, product, scrollY }) {
   194	  const [buyButtonLayout, setBuyButtonLayout] = useState(null);
   195	  const [imageLayout, setImageLayout] = useState(null);
   196	  const [lowerContentLayout, setLowerContentLayout] = useState(null);
   197	
   198	  const updateLayout = useCallback((setter) => ({ nativeEvent }) => {
   199	    const nextLayout = nativeEvent.layout;
   200	
   201	    setter((currentLayout) => {
   202	      if (
   203	        currentLayout &&
   204	        Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
   205	        Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
   206	      ) {
   207	        return currentLayout;
   208	      }
   209	
   210	      return nextLayout;
   211	    });
   212	  }, []);
   213	
   214	  return (
   215	    <CenterMagnifyView
   216	      magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayout}
   217	      magnifyExitAnchorLayout={buyButtonLayout || lowerContentLayout}
   218	      scrollY={scrollY}
   219	      style={productsStyles.productSectionWrap}
   220	    >
   221	      <ProductSection
   222	        croppedImageWidth={croppedImageWidth}
   223	        onBuyButtonLayout={updateLayout(setBuyButtonLayout)}
   224	        onBuyPress={onBuyPress}
   225	        onImageLayout={updateLayout(setImageLayout)}
   226	        onLowerContentLayout={updateLayout(setLowerContentLayout)}
   227	        product={product}
   228	      />
   229	    </CenterMagnifyView>
   230	  );
   231	}
   232	
   233	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   234	  const {
   235	    compactTopLayout,
   236	    initialContentOffset,
   237	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
   238	    scrollHandlers,
   239	    scrollY,
   240	  } =
   241	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
   242	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
   243	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   244	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
   245	  const openProductInShop = (productTitle) => {
   246	    router.push({
   247	      pathname: "/shop",
   248	      params: {
   249	        openProduct: String(Date.now()),
   250	        product: productTitle,
   251	      },
   252	    });
   253	  };
   254	
   255	  return (
   256	    <View style={productsStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   257	      <Animated.ScrollView
   258	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   259	        contentContainerStyle={[
   260	          productsStyles.scrollContent,
   261	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   262	        ]}
   263	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   264	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   265	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   266	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   267	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   268	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   269	        {...scrollHandlers}
   270	      >
   271	        <View style={productsStyles.main}>
   272	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   273	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   274	            pageTitleStyle={productsStyles.pageTitle}
   275	            scrollY={scrollY}
   276	          />
   277	
   278	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   279	            <Fragment key={product.title}>
   280	              <ProductMagnifySection
   281	                croppedImageWidth={croppedImageWidth}
   282	                onBuyPress={() => openProductInShop(product.title)}
   283	                product={product}
   284	                scrollY={scrollY}
   285	              />
   286	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   287	            </Fragment>
   288	          ))}
   289	        </View>
   290	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   291	    </View>
   292	  );
   293	}

FILE: mob/app/aboutus.js

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import { Animated, Image, Text, View, useWindowDimensions } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";

     6	import aboutusStyles from "../styles/aboutusStyles";
     7	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";

     9	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    10	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    11	
    12	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    13	const aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    14	const aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    15	
    16	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    17	  return (
    18	    currentLayout &&
    19	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    20	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    21	  );
    22	}
    23	
    24	export default function AboutusScreen() {
    25	  const [sectionLayouts, setSectionLayouts] = useState({});
    26	  const {
    27	    compactTopLayout,
    28	    initialContentOffset,
    29	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    30	    scrollHandlers,
    31	    scrollY,
    32	  } =
    33	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    34	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    35	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    36	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
    37	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    38	  const handleSectionLayout = useCallback(
    39	    (sectionName) =>
    40	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    41	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    42	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    43	
    44	        setSectionLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    45	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[sectionName], nextLayout)) {
    46	            return currentLayouts;
    47	          }
    48	
    49	          return {
    50	            ...currentLayouts,
    51	            [sectionName]: nextLayout,
    52	          };
    53	        });
    54	      },
    55	    []
    56	  );
    57	
    58	  return (
    59	    <View style={aboutusStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    60	      <Animated.ScrollView
    61	        style={aboutusStyles.scroll}
    62	        contentContainerStyle={[
    63	          aboutusStyles.scrollContent,
    64	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
    65	        ]}
    66	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
    67	        decelerationRate={0.95}
    68	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    69	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
    70	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
    71	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
    72	        {...scrollHandlers}
    73	      >
    74	        <View style={aboutusStyles.main}>
    75	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
    76	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
    77	            pageTitleStyle={aboutusStyles.pageTitle}
    78	            scrollY={scrollY}
    79	          />
    80	
    81	          <CenterMagnifyView
    82	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.image}
    83	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.image}
    84	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
    85	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
    86	            scrollY={scrollY}
    87	            style={aboutusStyles.aboutSection}
    88	          >
    89	            <View
    90	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("image")}
    91	              style={[
    92	                aboutusStyles.imageWrap,
    93	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
    94	              ]}
    95	            >
    96	              <Image
    97	                pointerEvents="none"
    98	                source={require("../convenience2_content_fill_mockup_1_plain_gray_gradient_mos9_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
    99	                style={[
   100	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   101	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   102	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   103	                ]}
   104	                resizeMode="contain"
   105	              />
   106	            </View>
   107	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   108	
   109	          <CenterMagnifyView
   110	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph1}
   111	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph1}
   112	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   113	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   114	            scrollY={scrollY}
   115	            style={[aboutusStyles.aboutSection, aboutusStyles.copy]}
   116	          >
   117	            <Text
   118	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("paragraph1")}
   119	              style={[
   120	                aboutusStyles.paragraph,
   121	                aboutusStyles.paragraphSectionGap,
   122	              ]}
   123	            >
   124	              Since we served up our very first cheeseplate, Alla Vostra has
   125	              been about one thing and one thing only: providing our customers
   126	              with the most intricate and delicious grazing boards you'll find
   127	              anywhere in the Miami metropolitan area.
   128	            </Text>
   129	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   130	
   131	          <CenterMagnifyView
   132	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph2}
   133	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph2}
   134	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   135	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   136	            scrollY={scrollY}
   137	            style={[aboutusStyles.aboutSection, aboutusStyles.copy]}
   138	          >
   139	            <Text
   140	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("paragraph2")}
   141	              style={[
   142	                aboutusStyles.paragraph,
   143	                aboutusStyles.paragraphSectionGap,
   144	              ]}
   145	            >
   146	              Whether ordering one of our products or all of them, you can count
   147	              on the fact that a purchase from our family-owned and operated
   148	              business is one whose taste will delight and memory bring joy long
   149	              after the last bite has been taken and the guests have gone home.
   150	            </Text>
   151	          </CenterMagnifyView>

   154	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph3}

   156	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   157	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}

   160	          >

FILE: mob/app/contact.js

     1	import { useState } from "react";
     2	import { Alert, Animated, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     5	import HapticPressable from "../components/HapticPressable";
     6	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     7	import contactStyles from "../styles/contactStyles";
     8	import { sendContactMessage } from "../utils/contactMessages";
     9	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    10	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    11	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    12	
    13	export default function ContactScreen() {
    14	  const [name, setName] = useState("");
    15	  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    16	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
    17	  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
    18	  const [emptyTouchedFieldKeys, setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys] = useState({});
    19	  const [blurredFieldKeys, setBlurredFieldKeys] = useState({});
    20	  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);
    21	  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState("");
    22	  const {
    23	    compactTopLayout,
    24	    initialContentOffset,
    25	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    26	    scrollHandlers,
    27	    scrollY,
    28	  } =
    29	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    30	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    31	  const trimmedName = name.trim();
    32	  const trimmedEmail = email.trim();
    33	  const trimmedMessage = message.trim();
    34	  const isEmailValid = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(trimmedEmail);
    35	  const canSubmit =
    36	    trimmedName.length > 0 &&
    37	    isEmailValid &&
    38	    trimmedMessage.length > 0 &&
    39	    !isSending;
    40	
    41	  const contactFieldValues = {
    42	    email,
    43	    message,
    44	    name,
    45	    phone,
    46	  };
    47	  const shouldShowFieldFault = (fieldKey) =>
    48	    Boolean(emptyTouchedFieldKeys[fieldKey]) ||
    49	    (fieldKey === "email" &&
    50	      Boolean(blurredFieldKeys.email) &&
    51	      trimmedEmail.length > 0 &&
    52	      !isEmailValid);
    53	
    54	  const clearFieldFault = (fieldKey) => {
    55	    setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    56	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    57	        return currentFieldKeys;
    58	      }
    59	
    60	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    61	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    62	      return nextFieldKeys;
    63	    });
    64	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    65	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    66	        return currentFieldKeys;
    67	      }
    68	
    69	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    70	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    71	      return nextFieldKeys;
    72	    });
    73	  };
    74	
    75	  const markFieldBlurred = (fieldKey) => {
    76	    const fieldValue = contactFieldValues[fieldKey] || "";
    77	
    78	    setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
    79	      ...currentFieldKeys,
    80	      [fieldKey]: true,
    81	    }));
    82	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    83	      const isFieldEmpty = fieldValue.trim().length === 0;
    84	
    85	      if (isFieldEmpty) {
    86	        return {
    87	          ...currentFieldKeys,
    88	          [fieldKey]: true,
    89	        };
    90	      }
    91	
    92	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    93	        return currentFieldKeys;
    94	      }
    95	
    96	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    97	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    98	      return nextFieldKeys;
    99	    });
   100	  };
   101	
   102	  const updateContactField = (fieldKey, text, setter) => {
   103	    setter(text);
   104	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
   105	      if (text.trim().length === 0 || !currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
   106	        return currentFieldKeys;
   107	      }
   108	
   109	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
   110	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
   111	      return nextFieldKeys;
   112	    });
   113	  };
   114	
   115	  const showContactAlert = (title, alertMessage) => {
   116	    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.alert === "function") {
   117	      window.alert(`${title}\n${alertMessage}`);
   118	      return;
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    Alert.alert(title, alertMessage);
   122	  };
   123	
   124	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
   125	    if (!canSubmit) {
   126	      setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
   127	        ...currentFieldKeys,
   128	        email: true,
   129	      }));
   130	      setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
   131	        ...currentFieldKeys,
   132	        ...(trimmedName.length === 0 ? { name: true } : {}),
   133	        ...(trimmedEmail.length === 0 ? { email: true } : {}),
   134	        ...(trimmedMessage.length === 0 ? { message: true } : {}),
   135	      }));
   136	      setStatusMessage("Please enter your name, email, and message.");
   137	      return;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    setIsSending(true);
   141	    setStatusMessage("");
   142	
   143	    try {
   144	      await sendContactMessage({
   145	        email: trimmedEmail,
   146	        message: trimmedMessage,
   147	        name: trimmedName,
   148	        phone: phone.trim(),
   149	      });
   150	      setName("");
   151	      setEmail("");
   152	      setPhone("");
   153	      setMessage("");
   154	      setBlurredFieldKeys({});
   155	      setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys({});
   156	      setStatusMessage("Message sent. We will respond within 48 hours.");
   157	      showContactAlert(
   158	        "Message sent",
   159	        "Thank you. Your message was sent to Alla Vostra.",
   160	      );
   161	    } catch (error) {
   162	      const errorMessage = error?.message || "Please try again.";
   163	      setStatusMessage(errorMessage);
   164	      showContactAlert("Message not sent", errorMessage);
   165	    } finally {
   166	      setIsSending(false);
   167	    }
   168	  };
   169	
   170	  return (
   171	    <View style={contactStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   172	      <Animated.ScrollView
   173	        style={contactStyles.scroll}
   174	        contentContainerStyle={[
   175	          contactStyles.scrollContent,
   176	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   177	        ]}
   178	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   179	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   180	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   181	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   182	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   183	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   184	        {...scrollHandlers}
   185	      >
   186	        <View style={contactStyles.main}>
   187	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   188	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   189	            pageTitleStyle={contactStyles.pageTitle}
   190	            scrollY={scrollY}
   191	          />
   192	
   193	          <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   194	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>Got a business inquiry?</Text>
   195	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>
   196	              General questions about our products?
   197	            </Text>
   198	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>
   199	              We strive to respond within 48 hours.
   200	            </Text>
   201	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   202	
   203	          <View style={contactStyles.formCard}>
   204	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   205	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Full Name</Text>
   206	              <TextInput
   207	                style={[
   208	                  contactStyles.input,
   209	                  shouldShowFieldFault("name") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   210	                ]}
   211	                placeholder="Your Name"
   212	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   213	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("name")}
   214	                onChangeText={(text) => updateContactField("name", text, setName)}
   215	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("name")}
   216	                returnKeyType="next"
   217	                textContentType="name"
   218	                value={name}
   219	              />
   220	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   221	
   222	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   223	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Email Address</Text>
   224	              <TextInput
   225	                style={[
   226	                  contactStyles.input,
   227	                  shouldShowFieldFault("email") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   228	                ]}
   229	                placeholder="you@company.com"
   230	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   231	                keyboardType="email-address"
   232	                autoCapitalize="none"
   233	                autoCorrect={false}
   234	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("email")}
   235	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   236	                  updateContactField("email", text, setEmail)
   237	                }
   238	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("email")}
   239	                returnKeyType="next"
   240	                textContentType="emailAddress"
   241	                value={email}
   242	              />
   243	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   244	
   245	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   246	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Phone Number</Text>
   247	              <TextInput
   248	                style={[
   249	                  contactStyles.input,
   250	                  shouldShowFieldFault("phone") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   251	                ]}
   252	                placeholder="+1 (555) 1234-567"
   253	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   254	                keyboardType="phone-pad"
   255	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("phone")}
   256	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   257	                  updateContactField("phone", text, setPhone)
   258	                }
   259	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("phone")}
   260	                returnKeyType="next"
   261	                textContentType="telephoneNumber"
   262	                value={phone}
   263	              />
   264	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   265	
   266	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   267	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Your Message</Text>
   268	              <TextInput
   269	                style={[
   270	                  contactStyles.input,
   271	                  contactStyles.messageInput,
   272	                  shouldShowFieldFault("message") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   273	                ]}
   274	                placeholder="Your Message"
   275	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   276	                multiline
   277	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("message")}
   278	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   279	                  updateContactField("message", text, setMessage)
   280	                }
   281	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("message")}
   282	                returnKeyType="default"
   283	                textAlignVertical="top"
   284	                value={message}
   285	              />
   286	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   287	
   288	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY} style={contactStyles.buttonWrap}>
   289	              <HapticPressable
   290	                accessibilityRole="button"
   291	                disabled={isSending}
   292	                onPress={handleSubmit}
   293	                style={({ pressed }) => [
   294	                  contactStyles.button,
   295	                  (!canSubmit || pressed) && contactStyles.buttonDimmed,
   296	                ]}
   297	              >
   298	                <Text style={contactStyles.buttonText}>
   299	                  {isSending ? "Sending..." : "Send Message"}
   300	                </Text>
   301	              </HapticPressable>
   302	              {statusMessage ? (
   303	                <Text style={contactStyles.statusText}>{statusMessage}</Text>
   304	              ) : null}
   305	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   306	          </View>
   307	        </View>
   308	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   309	    </View>
   310	  );
   311	}

    75	
    76	function getProductServingCount(description) {

    78	
    79	  if (!match) {
    80	    return "";
    81	  }
    82

    84	}
    85	
    86	function getRequestedOverlayProductName(value) {
    87	  const rawValue = Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value;
    88	  const requestedName = String(rawValue || "").trim();
    89	
    90	  if (!requestedName) {
    91	    return "";
    92	  }
    93	
    94	  const normalizedRequestedName = requestedName.toLowerCase();
    95	  const requestedProduct = products.find(
    96	    (product) => product.name.toLowerCase() === normalizedRequestedName,
    97	  );
    98	
    99	  return requestedProduct?.name || "";
   100	}
   101	
   102	function OptionOneButtonGradient({ variant }) {
   103	  const colorsByVariant = {
   104	    green: ["#2F9348", "#247C3A", "#1D6630"],
   105	    orange: orangeButtonGradientColors,
   106	    red: ["#DD3939", "#C62828", "#A92121"],
   107	    removeRed: ["#CF3128", "#B91F18", "#941913"],
   108	  };
   109	  const colors = colorsByVariant[variant] || colorsByVariant.green;
   110	
   111	  return (
   112	    <LinearGradient
   113	      colors={colors}
   114	      locations={[0, 0.52, 1]}
   115	      pointerEvents="none"
   116	      start={{ x: 0.5, y: 0 }}
   117	      end={{ x: 0.5, y: 1 }}
   118	      style={shopStyles.confirmationOverlayButtonGradient}
   119	    />
   120	  );
   121	}
   122	
   123	function renderOverlayDescription(description) {
   124	  const match = description.match(productServingLeadPattern);
   125	
   126	  if (!match) {
   127	    return description;
   128	  }
   129

   265	const getOverlayRequiredFieldKeys = (rows) =>
   266	  rows.reduce((fieldKeys, row) => {
   267	    row.forEach((field) => {
   268	      if (
   269	        field.type === "rowGapAfter" ||
   270	        field.type === "sectionHeading" ||
   271	        field.type === "spacer"
   272	      ) {
   273	        return;
   274	      }
   275	
   276	      if (field.type === "contactInfoBlock" || field.type === "fieldGroup") {
   277	        field.fields.forEach((groupField) => {
   278	          if (groupField.type === "spacer") {
   279	            return;
   280	          }
   281	
   282	          fieldKeys.push(groupField.key);
   283	        });
   284	        return;
   285	      }
   286	
   287	      if (field.type === "deliveryTimeWheels") {
   288	        fieldKeys.push(...deliveryTimeRequiredFieldKeys);
   289	        return;
   290	      }
   291	
   292	      fieldKeys.push(field.key);
   293	    });
   294	
   295	    return fieldKeys;
   296	  }, []);

   542	const isDeliveryServiceAreaZip = (zipValue) => {
   543	  const normalizedZip = normalizeDeliveryZip(zipValue);
   544	
   545	  if (normalizedZip.length !== 5) {
   546	    return false;
   547	  }
   548	
   549	  if (deliveryServiceAreaZipCodes.has(normalizedZip)) {
   550	    return true;
   551	  }
   552	
   553	  const numericZip = Number(normalizedZip);
   554	
   555	  return deliveryServiceAreaZipRanges.some(
   556	    ([startZip, endZip]) => numericZip >= startZip && numericZip <= endZip,
   557	  );
   558	};

   573	const getOrdinalDateLabel = (dateNumber) => {
   574	  const lastTwoDigits = dateNumber % 100;
   575	
   576	  if (lastTwoDigits >= 11 && lastTwoDigits <= 13) {
   577	    return `${dateNumber}th`;
   578	  }
   579	
   580	  switch (dateNumber % 10) {
   581	    case 1:
   582	      return `${dateNumber}st`;
   583	    case 2:
   584	      return `${dateNumber}nd`;
   585	    case 3:
   586	      return `${dateNumber}rd`;
   587	    default:
   588	      return `${dateNumber}th`;
   589	  }
   590	};

   652	function getStripeCardBrandLabel(brand) {
   653	  switch (brand) {
   654	    case "Visa":
   655	      return "VISA";
   656	    case "MasterCard":
   657	      return "MASTERCARD";
   658	    case "AmericanExpress":
   659	      return "AMEX";
   660	    case "Unknown":
   661	    case undefined:
   662	    case null:
   663	      return "";
   664	    default:
   665	      return String(brand)
   666	        .replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2")
   667	        .toUpperCase();
   668	  }

   851	
   852	function PiccolaQuantityTriangle({ direction, muted }) {
   853	  const strokeInset = piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth / 2;
   854	  const strokeColor = muted ? "#888888" : "#111111";
   855	  const isUpTriangle = direction === "up";
   856	  const fillGradientId = isUpTriangle
   857	    ? "piccolaQuantityTriangleTopGradient"
   858	    : "piccolaQuantityTriangleBottomGradient";
   859	  const fillGradientColors = isUpTriangle
   860	    ? topOverlayGradientColors
   861	    : orangeButtonGradientColors;
   862	  const path =
   863	    isUpTriangle
   864	      ? [
   865	          `M ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${strokeInset}`,
   866	          `L ${
   867	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   868	          } ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset}`,
   869	          `L ${strokeInset} ${
   870	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   871	          }`,
   872	          "Z",
   873	        ].join(" ")
   874	      : [
   875	          `M ${strokeInset} ${strokeInset}`,
   876	          `L ${
   877	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   878	          } ${strokeInset}`,
   879	          `L ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${
   880	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   881	          }`,
   882	          "Z",
   883	        ].join(" ");
   884	
   885	  return (
   886	    <Svg
   887	      height="100%"
   888	      style={shopStyles.piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleSvg}
   889	      viewBox={`0 0 ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth} ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}`}
   890	      width="100%"
   891	    >
   892	      <Defs>
   893	        <SvgLinearGradient
   894	          gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
   895	          id={fillGradientId}
   896	          x1={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2}
   897	          x2={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2}
   898	          y1={isUpTriangle ? piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight : 0}
   899	          y2={isUpTriangle ? 0 : piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}
   900	        >
   901	          {fillGradientColors.map((color, index) => (
   902	            <Stop
   903	              key={`${fillGradientId}-${color}`}
   904	              offset={`${index * 50}%`}
   905	              stopColor={color}
   906	            />
   907	          ))}
   908	        </SvgLinearGradient>
   909	      </Defs>
   910	      <Path
   911	        d={path}
   912	        fill={`url(#${fillGradientId})`}
   913	        opacity={muted ? 0.5 : 1}
   914	        stroke={strokeColor}
   915	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   916	        strokeWidth={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth}
   917	      />
   918	    </Svg>
   919	  );
   920	}

   970	function ShippingPreviewChromeCorners() {
   971	  return shippingPreviewChromeCorners.map((corner) => {
   972	    const gradientId = `shipping-preview-chrome-${corner.key}`;
   973	
   974	    return (
   975	      <View
   976	        key={corner.key}
   977	        pointerEvents="none"
   978	        style={[shopStyles.shippingPreviewItemButtonChromeCorner, corner.style]}
   979	      >
   980	        <Svg
   981	          height="100%"
   982	          preserveAspectRatio="none"
   983	          style={shopStyles.shippingPreviewItemButtonChromeFill}
   984	          viewBox="0 0 52 52"
   985	          width="100%"
   986	        >
   987	          <Defs>
   988	            <RadialGradient
   989	              cx={corner.cx}
   990	              cy={corner.cy}
   991	              fx={corner.cx}
   992	              fy={corner.cy}
   993	              id={gradientId}
   994	              r="108%"
   995	            >
   996	              {shippingPreviewChromeStops.map((stop) => (
   997	                <Stop
   998	                  key={stop.offset}
   999	                  offset={stop.offset}
  1000	                  stopColor={stop.color}
  1001	                />
  1002	              ))}
  1003	            </RadialGradient>
  1004	          </Defs>
  1005	          <Rect
  1006	            fill={`url(#${gradientId})`}
  1007	            height="52"
  1008	            width="52"
  1009	            x="0"
  1010	            y="0"
  1011	          />
  1012	        </Svg>
  1013	      </View>
  1014	    );
  1015	  });

  1306	      : isTimeDeliveryActionVisible
  1307	        ? "Who"
  1308	        : isDeliveryPaymentActionVisible
  1309	          ? "When"
  1310	          : isPaymentViewCartActionVisible
  1311	            ? "Where"
  1312	            : isPlaceholderActionVisible
  1313	              ? "Payment"
  1314	              : shippingPreviewActionAccessibilityLabel;

  1316	    (product) => (overlayProductQuantities[product.name] || 0) < 1,
  1317	  );

  1322	      (cartProduct) => cartProduct.name === product.name,
  1323	    ),
  1324	  );
  1325	  const shouldDimShippingPreviewRightAction =

  1340	    shippingPreviewActionLeftSideBoxWidth;

  1342	    shouldShowShippingPreviewActionSideBoxes
  1343	      ? shippingPreviewActionSideBoxBleed
  1344	      : 0;

  1346	    shippingPreviewActionBaseSideBoxBleed;

  1399	  const shippingPreviewActionBandIndex =
  1400	    isPaymentViewCartActionVisible || isPlaceholderActionVisible

  1509	  const triggerShopInteractionTick = (duration = 8, delay = 0) => {

  1511	  };
  1512	
  1513	  const triggerDeliveryTextFieldTick = () => {
  1514	    const now = Date.now();
  1515	
  1516	    if (now - lastDeliveryTextFieldTickAtRef.current < 180) {
  1517	      return;
  1518	    }
  1519	
  1520	    lastDeliveryTextFieldTickAtRef.current = now;
  1521	    triggerShopInteractionTick(18, 45);
  1522	  };
  1523	
  1524	  const activateDeliveryTextField = (fieldKey) => {
  1525	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(fieldKey);
  1526	    setEmptyTouchedDeliveryFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
  1527	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
  1528	        return currentFieldKeys;
  1529	      }
  1530	
  1531	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
  1532	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
  1533	      return nextFieldKeys;
  1534	    });
  1535	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1536	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1537	    setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(null);
  1538	    setIsPaymentIssuerDropdownOpen(false);
  1539	  };
  1540	
  1541	  const deactivateDeliveryTextField = (fieldKey) => {
  1542	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey((currentFieldKey) =>
  1543	      currentFieldKey === fieldKey ? null : currentFieldKey,
  1544	    );
  1545	    setEmptyTouchedDeliveryFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
  1546	      const fieldValue = deliveryFieldValues[fieldKey] || "";
  1547	      const isFieldEmpty = fieldValue.trim().length === 0;
  1548	
  1549	      if (isFieldEmpty) {
  1550	        return {
  1551	          ...currentFieldKeys,
  1552	          [fieldKey]: true,
  1553	        };
  1554	      }
  1555	
  1556	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
  1557	        return currentFieldKeys;
  1558	      }
  1559	
  1560	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
  1561	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
  1562	      return nextFieldKeys;
  1563	    });
  1564	
  1565	    if (
  1566	      isContactOverlayVisible &&
  1567	      fieldKey === "email" &&
  1568	      (deliveryFieldValues.email || "").trim().length > 0 &&
  1569	      !(deliveryFieldValues.email || "").includes("@")
  1570	    ) {
  1571	      shakeDeliveryOverlay();
  1572	    }
  1573	  };
  1574	
  1575	  const focusDeliveryTextField = (fieldKey) => {
  1576	    activateDeliveryTextField(fieldKey);
  1577	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  1578	      deliveryFieldInputRefs.current[fieldKey]?.focus?.();
  1579	    });
  1580	  };
  1581

  1644	      deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current.stop();
  1645	    }
  1646	
  1647	    deliveryOverlayShakeX.setValue(0);
  1648	    deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current = Animated.sequence([
  1649	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1650	        duration: 45,
  1651	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1652	        toValue: -8,
  1653	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1654	      }),
  1655	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1656	        duration: 45,
  1657	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1658	        toValue: 8,
  1659	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1660	      }),
  1661	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1662	        duration: 45,
  1663	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1664	        toValue: -6,
  1665	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1666	      }),
  1667	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1668	        duration: 45,
  1669	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1670	        toValue: 6,
  1671	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1672	      }),
  1673	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1674	        duration: 45,
  1675	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
  1676	        toValue: 0,
  1677	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1678	      }),
  1679	    ]);
  1680	    deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current.start(({ finished }) => {
  1681	      if (finished) {
  1682	        deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current = null;

  1684	    });

  1712	
  1713	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1714	      ...currentValues,

  1721	
  1722	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };

  1761	
  1762	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex = (options, optionIndex) =>
  1763	    deliveryTimeWheelLoopMidpoint * options.length + optionIndex;
  1764	
  1765	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY = (scrollY) =>
  1766	    Math.max(0, Math.round(scrollY / deliveryTimeWheelScrollStepHeight));
  1767	
  1768	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelOptionIndexFromScrollY = (options, scrollY) => {
  1769	    if (options.length === 0) {
  1770	      return 0;
  1771	    }
  1772	
  1773	    const loopedIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY(scrollY);
  1774	
  1775	    return ((loopedIndex % options.length) + options.length) % options.length;
  1776	  };
  1777	
  1778	  const setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex = (fieldKey, loopedIndex) => {
  1779	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleIndexes((currentIndexes) => {
  1780	      if (currentIndexes[fieldKey] === loopedIndex) {
  1781	        return currentIndexes;
  1782	      }
  1783	
  1784	      return {
  1785	        ...currentIndexes,
  1786	        [fieldKey]: loopedIndex,
  1787	      };
  1788	    });
  1789	  };
  1790	
  1791	  const triggerDeliveryTimeWheelTick = (fieldKey, loopedIndex) => {
  1792	    if (
  1793	      Platform.OS !== "android" ||
  1794	      !deliveryTimeWheelIsDraggingRef.current[fieldKey] ||
  1795	      deliveryTimeWheelHapticIndexesRef.current[fieldKey] === loopedIndex
  1796	    ) {
  1797	      return;
  1798	    }

  1800	    deliveryTimeWheelHapticIndexesRef.current[fieldKey] = loopedIndex;
  1801	    triggerShopInteractionTick();
  1802	  };
  1803	
  1804	  const updateDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleIndex = (fieldKey, scrollY) => {
  1805	    const loopedIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY(scrollY);
  1806	
  1807	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex(fieldKey, loopedIndex);
  1808	    triggerDeliveryTimeWheelTick(fieldKey, loopedIndex);
  1809	  };
  1810	
  1811	  const scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex = (
  1812	    fieldKey,
  1813	    optionIndex,
  1814	    options,
  1815	    animated = true,
  1816	  ) => {
  1817	    const offset =
  1818	      getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex(options, optionIndex) *
  1819	      deliveryTimeWheelScrollStepHeight;
  1820	
  1821	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  1822	      const scrollNode = deliveryTimeWheelScrollRefs.current[fieldKey];
  1823	
  1824	      if (scrollNode?.scrollToOffset) {
  1825	        scrollNode.scrollToOffset({ animated, offset });
  1826	        return;
  1827	      }
  1828	
  1829	      scrollNode?.scrollTo?.({
  1830	        animated,
  1831	        y: offset,
  1832	      });
  1833	    });
  1834	  };
  1835	
  1836	  const setDeliveryTimeWheelValue = (
  1837	    fieldKey,
  1838	    option,
  1839	    options,
  1840	    shouldScroll = true,
  1841	  ) => {
  1842	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1843	      ...currentValues,
  1844	      [fieldKey]: option,
  1845	    }));
  1846	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(fieldKey);
  1847	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1848	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1849	    setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(null);
  1850	    setIsPaymentIssuerDropdownOpen(false);
  1851	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex(
  1852	      fieldKey,
  1853	      getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex(
  1854	        options,
  1855	        Math.max(0, options.indexOf(option)),
  1856	      ),
  1857	    );
  1858	
  1859	    if (shouldScroll) {
  1860	      scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex(
  1861	        fieldKey,
  1862	        Math.max(0, options.indexOf(option)),
  1863	        options,
  1864	        true,
  1865	      );
  1866	    }
  1867	  };
  1868	
  1869	  const settleDeliveryTimeWheel = (fieldKey, options, scrollY) => {
  1870	    const optionIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelOptionIndexFromScrollY(
  1871	      options,
  1872	      scrollY,
  1873	    );
  1874	
  1875	    setDeliveryTimeWheelValue(fieldKey, options[optionIndex], options, false);
  1876	    deliveryTimeWheelIsDraggingRef.current[fieldKey] = false;
  1877	    scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex(fieldKey, optionIndex, options, false);
  1878	  };
  1879	
  1880	  const togglePaymentIssuerDropdown = () => {

  1895

  1975	        );

  1983	        );

  1991	        );

  1997	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  1998	          "This iPhone is not ready for Apple Pay. Make sure Wallet has an active card, then try again.",
  1999	        );
  2000	      }

  2002	      return;
  2003	    }
  2004

  2007	        "Google Pay unavailable",
  2008	        "Google Pay is only available on Android. Use Debit/Credit Card on this device.",
  2009	      );
  2010	      return;
  2011	    }
  2012	
  2013	    if (isExpoGo) {
  2014	      showPaymentAlert(
  2015	        "Google Pay needs a development build",
  2016	        "Expo Go cannot open the native Google Pay sheet. Use an Android development build or Play Store build to test Google Pay.",
  2017	      );
  2018	      return;
  2019	    }
  2020	
  2021	    if (!isGooglePaySupported) {
  2022	      showPaymentAlert(
  2023	        "Google Pay unavailable",
  2024	        "This device is not ready for Google Pay. Make sure Google Wallet is set up, then try again.",
  2025	      );

  2209	      !["Succeeded", "Processing"].includes(confirmResult.paymentIntent.status)

  2213	      );

  2236	      );

  2240	  const clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {
  2241	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2242	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2243	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
  2244	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2245	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2246	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);
  2247	  };
  2248	
  2249	  const startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
  2250	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2251	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2252	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2253	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2254	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
  2255	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
  2256	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
  2257	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0,
  2258	    );
  2259	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
  2260	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0,
  2261	    );
  2262	  };
  2263	
  2264	  const showOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
  2265	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2266	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
  2267	  };
  2268	
  2269	  const hideOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
  2270	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2271	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2272	  };
  2273	
  2274	  const overlayVisibleArrowOpacity = overlayHeldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
  2275	    inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
  2276	    outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
  2277	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2278	  });
  2279	  const overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
  2280	    overlayVisibleArrowOpacity,
  2281	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
  2282	      inputRange: [0, 1],
  2283	      outputRange: [1, 0],
  2284	      extrapolate: "clamp",
  2285	    }),
  2286	  );
  2287	  const overlayLeftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
  2288	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
  2289	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity,
  2290	  ).interpolate({
  2291	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2292	    outputRange: [0, 1],
  2293	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2294	  });
  2295	  const overlayRightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
  2296	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
  2297	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity,
  2298	  ).interpolate({
  2299	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2300	    outputRange: [0, 1],
  2301	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2302	  });
  2303	  const overlayImageTravelDistance = Math.max(
  2304	    overlayImageStageWidth / 2 + piccolaOverlayImageHalfSize,
  2305	    150,
  2306	  );
  2307	  const overlayIncomingStartOffset =
  2308	    overlayImageDirection < 0
  2309	      ? overlayImageTravelDistance
  2310	      : -overlayImageTravelDistance;
  2311	  const overlayOutgoingEndOffset = -overlayIncomingStartOffset;
  2312	  const overlayIncomingImageTranslateX = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2313	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2314	    outputRange: [overlayIncomingStartOffset, 0],
  2315	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2316	  });
  2317	  const overlayOutgoingImageTranslateX = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2318	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2319	    outputRange: [0, overlayOutgoingEndOffset],
  2320	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2321	  });
  2322	  const overlayIncomingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2323	    inputRange: [0, 0.52, 0.82, 1],
  2324	    outputRange: [0, 0.08, 0.68, 1],
  2325	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2326	  });
  2327	  const overlayOutgoingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2328	    inputRange: [0, 0.18, 0.36, 1],
  2329	    outputRange: [1, 0.22, 0, 0],
  2330	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2331	  });
  2332	
  2333	  const getOverlayProductTransitionDirection = (fromName, toName) => {
  2334	    const fromIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2335	      (product) => product.name === fromName,
  2336	    );
  2337	    const toIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2338	      (product) => product.name === toName,
  2339	    );
  2340	
  2341	    if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) {
  2342	      return -1;
  2343	    }
  2344	
  2345	    const lastOverlayIndex = overlayNavProducts.length - 1;
  2346	
  2347	    if (fromIndex === lastOverlayIndex && toIndex === 0) return -1;
  2348	    if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastOverlayIndex) return 1;
  2349	
  2350	    return toIndex > fromIndex ? -1 : 1;
  2351	  };
  2352	
  2353	  const transitionOverlayProductImage = (nextProductName, direction) => {
  2354	    if (!nextProductName || nextProductName === activeOverlayProductName) {
  2355	      return;
  2356	    }
  2357	
  2358	    if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
  2359	      const previousAnimation = overlayImageAnimationRef.current;
  2360	      overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  2361	      previousAnimation.stop();
  2362	    }
  2363	
  2364	    if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  2365	      const previousNavAnimation = overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current;
  2366	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  2367	      previousNavAnimation.stop();
  2368	    }
  2369	
  2370	    const nextOverlayNavIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2371	      (product) => product.name === nextProductName,
  2372	    );
  2373	
  2374	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  2375	    setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(activeOverlayProductName);
  2376	    setOverlayImageDirection(direction);
  2377	    overlayImageProgress.setValue(0);
  2378	    setActiveOverlayProductName(nextProductName);
  2379	
  2380	    if (nextOverlayNavIndex >= 0) {
  2381	      const navAnimation = Animated.timing(overlayNavIndicatorProgress, {
  2382	        toValue: nextOverlayNavIndex,
  2383	        duration: 320,
  2384	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  2385	        useNativeDriver: true,
  2386	      });
  2387	
  2388	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = navAnimation;
  2389	      navAnimation.start(() => {
  2390	        if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current === navAnimation) {
  2391	          overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  2392	        }
  2393	      });
  2394	    }
  2395	
  2396	    const animation = Animated.timing(overlayImageProgress, {
  2397	      toValue: 1,
  2398	      duration: 320,
  2399	      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  2400	      useNativeDriver: true,
  2401	    });
  2402	
  2403	    overlayImageAnimationRef.current = animation;
  2404	
  2405	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
  2406	      if (finished) {
  2407	        setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(null);
  2408	      }
  2409	
  2410	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current === animation) {
  2411	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  2412	      }
  2413	    });
  2414	  };
  2415	
  2416	  const handleOverlayProductNameSelect = (nextProductName) => {

  2418	      activeOverlayProductName,
  2419	      nextProductName,
  2420	    );
  2421

  2425	  const renderOverlayArrowChevron = (direction, muted = false) => (

  2437	  const goToOverlayPreviousProduct = () => {

  2461	  const getOverlayBandTouchPoint = (event) => {
  2462	    const primaryTouch =
  2463	      event?.nativeEvent?.touches?.[0] ||
  2464	      event?.nativeEvent?.changedTouches?.[0] ||
  2465	      event?.nativeEvent;
  2466	
  2467	    return {
  2468	      x:
  2469	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageX === "number"
  2470	          ? primaryTouch.pageX
  2471	          : primaryTouch?.locationX,
  2472	      y:
  2473	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageY === "number"
  2474	          ? primaryTouch.pageY
  2475	          : primaryTouch?.locationY,
  2476	    };
  2477	  };
  2478	
  2479	  const handleOverlayBandTouchStart = (event) => {
  2480	    const { x, y } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  2481	
  2482	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = x;
  2483	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = y;

  2491	    const startX = overlaySwipeStartXRef.current;
  2492	    const startY = overlaySwipeStartYRef.current;

  2494	
  2495	    if (
  2496	      typeof startX !== "number" ||
  2497	      typeof startY !== "number" ||

  2500	    ) {
  2501	      return;
  2502	    }

  2504	    const dx = currentX - startX;
  2505	    const dy = Math.abs(currentY - startY);
  2506	    const minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance = 12.5;
  2507	    const horizontalDominanceRatio = 0.4375;
  2508	    const isHorizontalSwipeIntent =
  2509	      Math.abs(dx) >= minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance &&
  2510	      Math.abs(dx) > dy * horizontalDominanceRatio;
  2511	
  2512	    if (!isHorizontalSwipeIntent) return;
  2513	
  2514	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = true;
  2515	
  2516	    if (dx < 0) {
  2517	      goToOverlayNextProduct();
  2518	    } else {
  2519	      goToOverlayPreviousProduct();
  2520	    }
  2521	  };

  3708	  const handleOrderConfirmationYesPress = async () => {
  3709	    if (isStripePaymentInFlight) return;
  3710	
  3711	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  3712	      showPaymentAlert(
  3713	        "Payment unavailable",
  3714	        "Stripe payments are available in the mobile app build.",
  3715	      );
  3716	      return;
  3717	    }
  3718	
  3719	    const configurationIssue = getStripeConfigurationIssue();
  3720	
  3721	    if (configurationIssue) {
  3722	      showPaymentAlert("Stripe setup needed", configurationIssue);
  3723	      return;
  3724	    }
  3725	
  3726	    if (
  3727	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayCardMethod &&
  3728	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod &&
  3729	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayApplePayMethod
  3730	    ) {
  3731	      showPaymentAlert(
  3732	        "Payment method unavailable",
  3733	        "Use Debit/Credit Card, Google Pay, or Apple Pay for this Stripe checkout.",
  3734	      );
  3735	      return;
  3736	    }
  3737	
  3738	    if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  3739	      if (Platform.OS !== "ios") {
  3740	        showPaymentAlert(
  3741	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  3742	          "Apple Pay is only available on iPhone. Use Debit/Credit Card or Google Pay on this device.",
  3743	        );
  3744	        return;
  3745	      }
  3746	
  3747	      if (isExpoGo) {
  3748	        showPaymentAlert(
  3749	          "Apple Pay needs a development build",
  3750	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Apple Pay sheet. Use an iOS development build or App Store build to test Apple Pay.",
  3751	        );
  3752	        return;
  3753	      }
  3754	
  3755	      if (!stripeMerchantIdentifier) {
  3756	        showPaymentAlert(
  3757	          "Apple Pay setup needed",
  3758	          "Add EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_MERCHANT_IDENTIFIER to mob/.env so StripeProvider can use your Apple merchant ID.",
  3759	        );
  3760	        return;
  3761	      }
  3762	
  3763	      if (!isApplePaySupported) {
  3764	        showPaymentAlert(
  3765	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  3766	          "This iPhone is not ready for Apple Pay. Make sure Wallet has an active card, then try again.",
  3767	        );
  3768	        return;
  3769	      }
  3770	    } else if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod) {
  3771	      if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  3772	        showPaymentAlert(
  3773	          "Google Pay unavailable",
  3774	          "Google Pay is only available on Android. Use Debit/Credit Card on this device.",
  3775	        );
  3776	        return;
  3777	      }
  3778	
  3779	      if (isExpoGo) {
  3780	        showPaymentAlert(
  3781	          "Google Pay needs a development build",
  3782	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Google Pay sheet. Use an Android development build or Play Store build to test Google Pay.",
  3783	        );
  3784	        return;
  3785	      }
  3786	
  3787	      if (!isGooglePaySupported) {
  3788	        showPaymentAlert(
  3789	          "Google Pay unavailable",
  3790	          "This device is not ready for Google Pay. Make sure Google Wallet is set up, then try again.",
  3791	        );
  3792	        return;
  3793	      }
  3794	    } else if (
  3795	      !isPaymentCardAccepted ||
  3796	      !stripeCardDetails?.complete ||
  3797	      !acceptedStripePaymentMethodId
  3798	    ) {
  3799	      showPaymentAlert(
  3800	        "Card details needed",
  3801	        "Enter a complete card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  3802	      );
  3803	      return;
  3804	    }
  3805	
  3806	    const orderPayload = buildStripeOrderPayload();
  3807	
  3808	    if (orderPayload.items.length === 0) {
  3809	      showPaymentAlert("Cart is empty", "Add an item before placing an order.");
  3810	      return;
  3811	    }
  3812	
  3813	    setIsStripePaymentInFlight(true);
  3814	
  3815	    try {
  3816	      const paymentSheet = await createStripePaymentSheet(orderPayload);
  3817	      if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  3818	        await confirmApplePayPayment(
  3819	          paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret,
  3820	          paymentSheet,
  3821	        );
  3822	      } else if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod) {
  3823	        await confirmGooglePayPayment(paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret);
  3824	      } else {
  3825	        const confirmResult = await confirmPayment(
  3826	          paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret,
  3827	          {
  3828	            paymentMethodType: "Card",
  3829	            paymentMethodData: {
  3830	              billingDetails: buildStripeBillingDetails(),
  3831	              paymentMethodId: acceptedStripePaymentMethodId,
  3832	            },
  3833	          },
  3834	        );
  3835	
  3836	        if (confirmResult.error) {
  3837	          throw new Error(
  3838	            confirmResult.error.localizedMessage ||
  3839	              confirmResult.error.message ||
  3840	              "Payment was not completed.",
  3841	          );
  3842	        }
  3843	
  3844	        if (
  3845	          confirmResult.paymentIntent?.status &&
  3846	          !["Succeeded", "Processing"].includes(
  3847	            confirmResult.paymentIntent.status,
  3848	          )
  3849	        ) {
  3850	          throw new Error(
  3851	            `Payment status is ${confirmResult.paymentIntent.status}. Please try again.`,
  3852	          );
  3853	        }
  3854	      }
  3855	
  3856	      showOrderPlacementConfirmation();
  3857	    } catch (error) {
  3858	      showPaymentAlert(
  3859	        "Payment not completed",
  3860	        error?.message || "Please try again.",
  3861	      );
  3862	    } finally {
  3863	      setIsStripePaymentInFlight(false);
  3864	    }
  3865	  };
  3866	  const handleShippingPreviewActionPress = () => {

  3977

  4000

  4106	  useEffect(() => {

  4128	            setIsApplePaySupported(false);
  4129	          }

  4136	
  4137	        return;

  4144	          if (isMounted) {
  4145	            setIsApplePaySupported(Boolean(isSupported));
  4146	            setIsGooglePaySupported(false);
  4147	          }
  4148	        } catch {
  4149	          if (isMounted) {
  4150	            setIsApplePaySupported(false);
  4151	            setIsGooglePaySupported(false);
  4152	          }
  4153	        }
  4154	
  4155	        return;
  4156	      }
  4157	
  4158	      if (isMounted) {
  4159	        setIsApplePaySupported(false);
  4160	        setIsGooglePaySupported(false);
  4161	      }
  4162	    };
  4163	
  4164	    checkPlatformPaySupport();
  4165	
  4166	    return () => {
  4167	      isMounted = false;

  4169	  }, [isPlatformPaySupported]);
  4170	
  4171	  useEffect(() => {
