  8463	            style={[
  8464	              shopStyles.deliveryOverlayStateDropdown,
  8465	              {
  8466	                height: paymentIssuerDropdownHeight,
  8467	                left: paymentIssuerDropdownAnchor.x,
  8468	                top: paymentIssuerDropdownTop,
  8469	                width: paymentIssuerDropdownAnchor.width,
  8470	              },
  8471	            ]}
  8472	          >
  8473	            {paymentIssuerOptions.map((option) => (
  8474	              <Pressable
  8475	                accessibilityLabel={`Select ${option}`}
  8476	                accessibilityRole="button"
  8477	                key={option}
  8478	                onPress={() => selectPaymentIssuerOption(option)}
  8479	                style={({ pressed }) => [
  8480	                  shopStyles.deliveryOverlayStateOption,
  8481	                  (pressed || selectedPaymentCardIssuer === option) &&
  8482	                    shopStyles.deliveryOverlayStateOptionSelected,
  8483	                ]}
  8484	              >
  8485	                <Text
  8486	                  allowFontScaling={false}
  8487	                  ellipsizeMode="clip"
  8488	                  numberOfLines={1}
  8489	                  style={[
  8490	                    shopStyles.deliveryOverlayStateOptionText,
  8491	                    selectedPaymentCardIssuer === option &&
  8492	                      shopStyles.deliveryOverlayStateOptionTextSelected,
  8493	                  ]}
  8494	                >
  8495	                  {option}
  8496	                </Text>
  8497	              </Pressable>
  8498	            ))}
  8499	          </View>
  8500	        </View>
  8501	      ) : null}
  8502	
  8503	      {isTruckOverlayVisible
  8504	        ? renderShippingPreviewActionButton({
  8505	            frameStyle: [
  8506	              shopStyles.shippingPreviewReadyButtonLiftFrame,
  8507	              {
  8508	                top: shippingPreviewActionButtonScreenTop,
  8509	                left: shippingPreviewActionClusterLeft,
  8510	              },
  8511	            ],
  8512	          })
  8513	        : null}
  8514	
  8515	      {isTruckOverlayVisible ? (
  8516	        <View
  8517	          pointerEvents="box-none"
  8518	          style={[
  8519	            shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftFrame,
  8520	            {
  8521	              bottom: safeAreaInsets.bottom + stickyCartEdgeOffset,
  8522	            },
  8523	          ]}
  8524	        >
  8525	            <ButtonShadowPlate
  8526	              style={shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftShadowPlate}
  8527	            />
  8528	            <Pressable
  8529	              accessibilityLabel={
  8530	                isPaymentOverlayVisible &&
  8531	                (isPaymentCardDetailsOverlayVisible ||
  8532	                  isPaymentPayPalOverlayVisible)
  8533	                  ? "Close payment popup"
  8534	                  : "Reset checkout and return to shop preview"
  8535	              }
  8536	              accessibilityRole="button"
  8537	              onPress={handleShopOverlayStickyLeftPress}
  8538	              style={shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftButton}
  8539	            >
  8540	            <OptionOneButtonGradient variant="orange" />
  8541	            <View
  8542	              pointerEvents="none"
  8543	              style={shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftX}
  8544	            >
  8545	              <View
  8546	                style={[
  8547	                  shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftXStroke,
  8548	                  shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftXStrokeForward,
  8549	                ]}
  8550	              />
  8551	              <View
  8552	                style={[
  8553	                  shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftXStroke,
  8554	                  shopStyles.shopOverlayStickyLeftXStrokeBack,
  8555	                ]}
  8556	              />
  8557	            </View>
  8558	          </Pressable>
  8559	        </View>
  8560	      ) : null}
  8561	    </View>
  8562	  );
  8563	}

    94	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    95	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    96	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    97	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
    98	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
    99	  return "home";
   100	}
   101	
   102	function getNavigationDirection(fromPage, toPage) {
   103	  const fromIndex = navPages.indexOf(fromPage);
   104	  const toIndex = navPages.indexOf(toPage);
   105	
   106	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) return 1;
   107	
   108	  const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   109	
   110	  if (fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0) return 1;
   111	  if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex) return -1;
   112	
   113	  return toIndex > fromIndex ? 1 : -1;
   114	}

   116	export default function AppHeader({
   117	  activePage,
   118	  dimHeaderExceptShopButton = false,
   119	  scrollY,
   120	  showCarousel = true,
   121	  showHero = true,
   122	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
   123	  showOnlyHero = false,
   124	}) {
   125	  const pathname = usePathname();
   126	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   127	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   128	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
   129	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   130	  const activePageIndex = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
   131	  const handlesCarouselVisuals = showCarousel && !showOnlyHero;
   132	
   133	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   134	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
   135	  const canNavigateWithHeader = useHeaderNavigationGate(safeScrollY);
   136	  const heroStateScrollY =
   137	    showHero && backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen && backgroundHeroState?.heroScrollY
   138	      ? backgroundHeroState.heroScrollY
   139	      : safeScrollY;
   140	  const headerMotionScrollY = showOnlyHero ? heroStateScrollY : safeScrollY;
   141	  const frozenHeroMotionScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   142	  const frozenHeaderMotionScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   143	  const shouldFreezeHeaderMotion = Boolean(
   144	    screenSwipe?.isActive || screenSwipe?.routeTransitionState?.isActive
   145	  );
   146	  const previousShouldFreezeHeaderMotionRef = useRef(false);
   147	
   148	  useEffect(() => {
   149	    if (
   150	      shouldFreezeHeaderMotion &&
   151	      !previousShouldFreezeHeaderMotionRef.current
   152	    ) {
   153	      frozenHeroMotionScrollY.setValue(readAnimatedValue(heroStateScrollY));
   154	      frozenHeaderMotionScrollY.setValue(readAnimatedValue(headerMotionScrollY));
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    previousShouldFreezeHeaderMotionRef.current = shouldFreezeHeaderMotion;
   158	  }, [
   159	    frozenHeroMotionScrollY,
   160	    frozenHeaderMotionScrollY,
   161	    headerMotionScrollY,
   162	    heroStateScrollY,
   163	    shouldFreezeHeaderMotion,
   164	  ]);
   165	
   166	  const resolvedHeroMotionScrollY = shouldFreezeHeaderMotion
   167	    ? frozenHeroMotionScrollY
   168	    : heroStateScrollY;
   169	  const resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY = shouldFreezeHeaderMotion
   170	    ? frozenHeaderMotionScrollY
   171	    : headerMotionScrollY;
   172	
   173	  const fallbackHeldArrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   174	  const fallbackRouteTransitionProgress = useRef(
   175	    new Animated.Value(0)
   176	  ).current;
   177	  const heldArrowOpacity =
   178	    screenSwipe?.heldArrowOpacity || fallbackHeldArrowOpacity;
   179	  const routeTransitionProgress =
   180	    screenSwipe?.routeTransitionProgress || fallbackRouteTransitionProgress;
   181	  const indicatorProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(activePageIndex)).current;
   182	  const fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity = useRef(
   183	    new Animated.Value(0)
   184	  ).current;
   185	  const fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity = useRef(
   186	    new Animated.Value(0)
   187	  ).current;
   188	  const fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression = useRef(
   189	    new Animated.Value(0)
   190	  ).current;
   191	  const directionalLeftArrowOpacity =
   192	    screenSwipe?.directionalLeftArrowOpacity ||
   193	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity;
   194	  const directionalRightArrowOpacity =
   195	    screenSwipe?.directionalRightArrowOpacity ||
   196	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity;
   197	  const directionalArrowBaseSuppression =
   198	    screenSwipe?.directionalArrowBaseSuppression ||
   199	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression;
   200	
   201	  const indicatorAnimationRef = useRef(null);
   202	  const indicatorIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   203	  const suppressCarouselPressUntilRef = useRef(0);
   204	  const canNavigateWithHeaderRef = useRef(canNavigateWithHeader);
   205	
   206	  const activePageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
   207	  const activeIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   208	  canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current = canNavigateWithHeader;
   209	  activePageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   210	  activeIndexRef.current = activePageIndex;
   211	
   212	  useEffect(() => {
   213	    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
   214	
   215	    const resolvedCheeseboardImage = Image.resolveAssetSource(
   216	      cheeseboardProductsImage
   217	    );
   218	
   219	    if (resolvedCheeseboardImage?.uri) {
   220	      Image.prefetch(resolvedCheeseboardImage.uri).catch(() => {});
   221	    }
   222	  }, []);
   223	
   224	  const showHeldArrows = (event = null) => {
   225	    if (screenSwipe?.showHeldArrowHint) {
   226	      screenSwipe.showHeldArrowHint(event);
   227	      return;
   228	    }
   229	
   230	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   231	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
   232	  };
   233	
   234	  const hideHeldArrows = () => {
   235	    if (screenSwipe?.hideHeldArrowHint) {
   236	      screenSwipe.hideHeldArrowHint();
   237	      return;
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   241	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   242	  };
   243	
   244	  const cancelHeldArrows = () => {
   245	    if (screenSwipe?.cancelHeldArrowHint) {
   246	      screenSwipe.cancelHeldArrowHint();
   247	      return;
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    hideHeldArrows();
   251	  };
   252	
   253	  const updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove = (event) => {
   254	    screenSwipe?.updateHeldArrowHintMovement?.(event);
   255	    return false;
   256	  };
   257	
   258	  const clearDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {
   259	    if (screenSwipe?.clearDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   260	      screenSwipe.clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   261	      return;
   262	    }
   263	
   264	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   265	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   266	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   267	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   268	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   269	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);
   270	  };
   271	
   272	  const startDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
   273	    if (screenSwipe?.startDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   274	      screenSwipe.startDirectionalArrowLinger(direction);
   275	      return;
   276	    }
   277	
   278	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   279	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   280	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   281	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
   282	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
   283	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   284	    );
   285	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
   286	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   287	    );
   288	  };
   289	
   290	  const updateSwipePreview = (dragDistance) => {
   291	    if (!screenSwipe) return;
   292	
   293	    screenSwipe.updateSwipe({
   294	      x: dragDistance,
   295	    });
   296	  };
   297	
   298	  const animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes = (fromIndex, toIndex) => {
   299	    if (fromIndex === toIndex) {
   300	      if (!indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   301	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   302	      }
   303	      return;
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    if (indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   307	      const previousAnimation = indicatorAnimationRef.current;
   308	      indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   309	      previousAnimation.stop();
   310	    }
   311	
   312	    indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   313	
   314	    const createIndicatorTiming = (toValue, duration = indicatorSlideDuration) =>
   315	      Animated.timing(indicatorProgress, {
   316	        toValue,
   317	        duration,
   318	        useNativeDriver: true,
   319	      });
   320	
   321	    const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   322	    const animation =
   323	      fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0
   324	        ? Animated.sequence([
   325	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length),
   326	            createIndicatorTiming(-1, 0),
   327	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   328	          ])
   329	        : fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex
   330	        ? Animated.sequence([
   331	            createIndicatorTiming(-1),
   332	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length, 0),
   333	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   334	          ])
   335	        : createIndicatorTiming(toIndex);
   336	
   337	    indicatorAnimationRef.current = animation;
   338	
   339	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   340	      if (finished) {
   341	        indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   342	      }
   343	
   344	      if (indicatorAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   345	        indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   346	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   347	      }
   348	    });
   349	  };
   350	
   351	  useEffect(() => {
   352	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(indicatorIndexRef.current, activePageIndex);
   353	  }, [activePageIndex, indicatorProgress]);
   354	
   355	  const animateIndicatorToPage = (pageName) => {
   356	    const pageIndex = navPages.indexOf(pageName);
   357	    if (pageIndex < 0) return;
   358	
   359	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(activeIndexRef.current, pageIndex);
   360	  };
   361	
   362	  const goToPage = (pageName, animateIndicator = true) => {
   363	    hideHeldArrows();
   364	
   365	    if (pageName === activePageRef.current) {
   366	      if (pageName === "shop") {
   367	        goToPage("home", animateIndicator);
   368	      }
   369	
   370	      return;
   371	    }
   372	
   373	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
   374	    if (!route) return;
   375	
   376	    if (navPages.includes(pageName)) {
   377	      if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   378	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   379	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   380	        return;
   381	      }
   382	
   383	      backgroundHeroState?.freezeHero(safeScrollY);
   384	      if (animateIndicator) animateIndicatorToPage(pageName);
   385	      router.replace(route);
   386	      return;
   387	    }
   388	
   389	    router.push(route);
   390	  };
   391	
   392	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
   393	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   394	
   395	    const previousIndex =
   396	      (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   397	
   398	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex], true, -1);
   399	  };
   400	
   401	  const goToNextPage = () => {
   402	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   403	
   404	    const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   405	
   406	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex], true, 1);
   407	  };
   408	
   409	  const shouldClaimCarouselSwipe = (_, gestureState) => {
   410	    if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return false;
   411	
   412	    const horizontalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dx);
   413	    const verticalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dy);
   414	
   415	    if (
   416	      horizontalDistance >= tapHoldHorizontalCancelDistance &&
   417	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * tapHoldHorizontalDominanceRatio
   418	    ) {
   419	      cancelHeldArrows();
   420	    }
   421	
   422	    const shouldClaim =
   423	      horizontalDistance > carouselSwipeActivationDistance &&
   424	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * carouselSwipeActivationRatio;
   425	
   426	    if (shouldClaim) {
   427	      cancelHeldArrows();
   428	    }
   429	
   430	    return shouldClaim;
   431	  };
   432	
   433	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
   434	    PanResponder.create({
   435	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   436	
   437	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   438	
   439	      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: (_, gestureState) =>
   440	        !shouldClaimCarouselSwipe(null, gestureState),
   441	
   442	      onPanResponderMove: (_, gestureState) => {
   443	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return;
   444	
   445	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   446	        updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   447	
   448	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   449	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   450	          return;
   451	        }
   452	
   453	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   454	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   455	          return;
   456	        }
   457	
   458	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   459	      },
   460	
   461	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
   462	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   463	        hideHeldArrows();
   464	
   465	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   466	          clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   467	          screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   468	          return;
   469	        }
   470	
   471	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   472	          const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   473	          const nextPage = navPages[nextIndex];
   474	
   475	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   476	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   477	            x: gestureState.dx,
   478	            page: nextPage,
   479	            direction: 1,
   480	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   481	          });
   482	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   483	          goToPage(nextPage, true, 1);
   484	
   485	          return;
   486	        }
   487	
   488	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   489	          const previousIndex =
   490	            (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   491	          const previousPage = navPages[previousIndex];
   492	
   493	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   494	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   495	            x: gestureState.dx,
   496	            page: previousPage,
   497	            direction: -1,
   498	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   499	          });
   500	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   501	          goToPage(previousPage, true, -1);
   502	
   503	          return;
   504	        }
   505	
   506	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   507	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   508	      },
   509	
   510	      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
   511	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   512	        hideHeldArrows();
   513	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   514	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   515	      },
   516	
   517	      onShouldBlockNativeResponder: () => true,
   518	    })
   519	  ).current;
   520	
   521	  useEffect(() => {
   522	    hideHeldArrows();
   523	
   524	    return () => {
   525	      hideHeldArrows();
   526	    };
   527	  }, [resolvedActivePage, showCarousel, showOnlyHero]);
   528	
   529	  useEffect(
   530	    () => () => {
   531	      clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   532	    },
   533	    []
   534	  );
   535	
   536	  const activeLink = pageLabels[resolvedActivePage] || "Home";
   537	  const arrowLinkTranslateX = 0;
   538	
   539	  const scrollBeganArrowVisibility = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   540	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   541	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   542	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   543	  });
   544	  const oldHeaderArrowVisibility = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   545	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   546	    outputRange: [1, 0],
   547	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   548	  });
   549	  const oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   550	    heldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   551	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   552	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   553	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   554	    }),
   555	    oldHeaderArrowVisibility
   556	  );
   557	  const scrolledStateArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   558	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility,
   559	    arrowHintPeakOpacity
   560	  );
   561	  const scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   562	    heldArrowOpacity,
   563	    scrolledStateArrowOpacity
   564	  );
   565	  const gatedScrolledArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   566	    scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   567	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   568	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   569	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   570	    }),
   571	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility
   572	  );
   573	  const gatedVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   574	    oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity,
   575	    gatedScrolledArrowOpacity
   576	  ).interpolate({
   577	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   578	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   579	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   580	  });
   581	  const directionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   582	    gatedVisibleArrowOpacity,
   583	    directionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
   584	      inputRange: [0, 1],
   585	      outputRange: [1, 0],
   586	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   587	    })
   588	  );
   589	  const leftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   590	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   591	    directionalLeftArrowOpacity
   592	  ).interpolate({
   593	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   594	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   595	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   596	  });
   597	  const rightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   598	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   599	    directionalRightArrowOpacity
   600	  ).interpolate({
   601	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   602	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   603	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   604	  });
   605	
   606	  const heroVerticalFadeTranslateY = resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   607	    inputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeScrollDistance],
   608	    outputRange: [heroVerticalFadeHeight, -heroVerticalFadeFeatherHeight],
   609	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   610	  });
   611	
   612	  const stickyOffset =
   613	    Platform.OS === "web"
   614	      ? 0
   615	      : resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   616	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   617	          outputRange: [0, carouselStickyOffsetY],
   618	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   619	        });
   620	  const carouselExpansionOffsetY =
   621	    Platform.OS === "ios"
   622	      ? resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   623	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   624	          outputRange: [0, carouselExpansionHeight],
   625	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   626	        })
   627	      : stickyOffset;
   628	  const carouselInnerTranslateY = carouselExpansionOffsetY;
   629	
   630	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   631	    Platform.OS === "web"
   632	      ? 1
   633	      : resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   634	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   635	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   636	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   637	        });
   638	
   639	  const stickyExpansionTranslateY = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   640	    inputRange: [96, 120],
   641	    outputRange: [-stickyExpansionMaxHeight, 0],
   642	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   643	  });
   644	
   645	  const heroScale = resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   646	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   647	    outputRange: [heroStartScale, heroScaleAtMinimumOpacity],
   648	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   649	  });
   650	
   651	  const heroTranslateY = resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   652	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   653	    outputRange: [heroStartTranslateY, heroTranslateYAtMinimumOpacity],
   654	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   655	  });
   656	
   657	  const headerTranslateY = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   658	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   659	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   660	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   661	  });
   662	
   663	  const indicatorSegmentWidth = windowWidth / navPages.length;
   664	  const indicatorTranslateX = Animated.multiply(
   665	    indicatorProgress,
   666	    indicatorSegmentWidth
   667	  );
   668	  const isMainHeroPage = navPages.includes(resolvedActivePage);
   669	  const heroImageSource = isMainHeroPage
   670	    ? noCheeseboardHeroImage
   671	    : defaultHeroImage;
   672	  const mainHeroVisualOpacity = isMainHeroPage
   673	    ? resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   674	        inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   675	        outputRange: [1, heroMinimumScrollOpacity],
   676	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   677	      })
   678	    : 1;
   679	  const heroWidthFitScale = windowWidth / heroSourceWidth;
   680	  const heroWidthFitImageHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroWidthFitScale;
   681	  const heroImageFrameStyle = isMainHeroPage
   682	    ? {
   683	        width: windowWidth,
   684	        height: heroWidthFitImageHeight,
   685	      }
   686	    : null;
   687	  const heroImageTransformStyle = isMainHeroPage
   688	    ? null
   689	    : {
   690	        transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   691	      };
   692	  const heroImageOpacityStyle = isMainHeroPage
   693	    ? { opacity: mainHeroVisualOpacity }
   694	    : null;
   695	  const heroCoverScale = Math.max(
   696	    windowWidth / heroSourceWidth,
   697	    heroVerticalFadeHeight / heroSourceHeight
   698	  );
   699	  const heroLayoutScale = isMainHeroPage ? heroWidthFitScale : heroCoverScale;
   700	  const heroRenderedWidth = heroSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   701	  const heroRenderedHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   702	  const heroRenderedLeft = (windowWidth - heroRenderedWidth) / 2;
   703	  const heroRenderedTop = isMainHeroPage
   704	    ? 0
   705	    : (heroVerticalFadeHeight - heroRenderedHeight) / 2;
   706	  const noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY = Math.min(
   707	    heroVerticalFadeHeight,
   708	    heroRenderedTop + noCheeseboardVisibleBottomSourceY * heroLayoutScale
   709	  );
   710	  const cheeseboardWidth = cheeseboardSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   711	  const cheeseboardHeight = cheeseboardSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   712	  const cheeseboardLeft =
   713	    heroRenderedLeft + cheeseboardSourceX * heroLayoutScale;
   714	  const cheeseboardTop = isMainHeroPage
   715	    ? noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY - cheeseboardHeight / 2
   716	    : heroRenderedTop + cheeseboardSourceY * heroLayoutScale;
   717	  const routeTransitionState = screenSwipe?.routeTransitionState;
   718	  const isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled =
   719	    isMainHeroPage && isHeaderNewState(resolvedHeroMotionScrollY);
   720	  const isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive =
   721	    isMainHeroPage &&
   722	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   723	    Boolean(routeTransitionState?.isActive);
   724	  const isCheeseboardSwipeActive =
   725	    isMainHeroPage &&
   726	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   727	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive);
   728	  const cheeseboardTransitionDirection =
   729	    routeTransitionState?.direction === -1 ? -1 : 1;
   730	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartX =
   731	    typeof routeTransitionState?.startX === "number"
   732	      ? routeTransitionState.startX
   733	      : 0;
   734	  const cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX =
   735	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.01 : 0;
   736	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX =
   737	    Math.abs(cheeseboardTransitionStartX) > 0.01
   738	      ? cheeseboardTransitionStartX
   739	      : cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX;
   740	  const cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity =
   741	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.001 : 0;
   742	  const cheeseboardDragTranslateX = isCheeseboardSwipeActive
   743	    ? screenSwipe?.swipeX || 0
   744	    : 0;
   745	  const cheeseboardBaseTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   746	    ? 0
   747	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   748	  const cheeseboardBaseOpacity = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   749	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   750	        inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   751	        outputRange: [
   752	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   753	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   754	          1,
   755	        ],
   756	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   757	      })
   758	    : 1;
   759	  const cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   760	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   761	        inputRange: [0, 1],
   762	        outputRange: [
   763	          cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX,
   764	          -cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   765	        ],
   766	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   767	      })
   768	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   769	  const cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   770	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   771	    outputRange: [
   772	      cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX +
   773	        cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   774	      cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX,
   775	    ],
   776	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   777	  });
   778	  const cheeseboardIncomingOpacity = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   779	    inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   780	    outputRange: [1, 1, 0],
   781	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   782	  });
   783	  const cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   784	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, -windowWidth)
   785	    : -windowWidth;
   786	  const cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   787	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, windowWidth)
   788	    : windowWidth;
   789	  const shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews =
   790	    isMainHeroPage &&
   791	    isCheeseboardSwipeActive &&
   792	    !isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive;
   793	  const handleCarouselTouchStart = (event) => {
   794	    showHeldArrows(event);
   795	    return false;
   796	  };
   797	
   798	  const sharedHeaderTouchHandlers = {
   799	    onStartShouldSetResponderCapture: handleCarouselTouchStart,
   800	    onTouchStart: showHeldArrows,
   801	    onTouchMove: updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove,
   802	    onTouchEnd: hideHeldArrows,
   803	    onTouchCancel: hideHeldArrows,
   804	  };
   805	
   806	  const renderArrowChevron = (direction) => (
   807	    <View
   808	      style={[
   809	        styles.arrowChevron,
   810	        direction === "left"
   811	          ? styles.arrowChevronLeft
   812	          : styles.arrowChevronRight,
   813	      ]}
   814	    />
   815	  );
   816	
   817	  const renderCheeseboardLayer = (key, translateX, opacity = 1) => {
   818	    const slideTransformStyle =
   819	      translateX === 0 ? null : { transform: [{ translateX }] };
   820	    const resolvedSlideOpacity =
   821	      isMainHeroPage && opacity !== 1
   822	        ? Animated.multiply(opacity, mainHeroVisualOpacity)
   823	        : isMainHeroPage
   824	        ? mainHeroVisualOpacity
   825	        : opacity;
   826	    const slideOpacityStyle =
   827	      resolvedSlideOpacity === 1 ? null : { opacity: resolvedSlideOpacity };
   828	
   829	    return (
   830	      <Animated.View
   831	        key={key}
   832	        collapsable={false}
   833	        pointerEvents="none"
   834	        renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   835	        style={[
   836	          styles.cheeseboardSlideLayer,
   837	          slideTransformStyle,
   838	          slideOpacityStyle,
   839	        ]}
   840	      >
   841	        <Animated.View
   842	          collapsable={false}
   843	          style={styles.cheeseboardHeroTransformLayer}
   844	        >
   845	          <Animated.View
   846	            collapsable={false}
   847	            renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   848	            style={[
   849	              styles.cheeseboardProductsFrame,
   850	              {
   851	                left: cheeseboardLeft,
   852	                top: cheeseboardTop,
   853	                width: cheeseboardWidth,
   854	                height: cheeseboardHeight,
   855	                transform: [{ scale: heroScale }],
   856	              },
   857	            ]}
   858	          >
   859	            <Image
   860	              fadeDuration={0}
   861	              source={cheeseboardProductsImage}
   862	              style={styles.cheeseboardProductsImage}
   863	              resizeMode="contain"
   864	            />
   865	          </Animated.View>
   866	        </Animated.View>
   867	      </Animated.View>
   868	    );
   869	  };
   870	
   871	  const carousel = (
   872	    <View
   873	      style={styles.carouselShell}
   874	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   875	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   876	    >
   877	      <Animated.View style={styles.carouselNavBar}>
   878	        <View style={styles.carouselStickyExpansion}>
   879	          <Animated.View
   880	            pointerEvents="none"
   881	            style={[
   882	              styles.carouselStickyExpansionFill,
   883	              {
   884	                transform: [{ translateY: stickyExpansionTranslateY }],
   885	              },
   886	            ]}
   887	          />
   888	        </View>
   889	
   890	        <Animated.View
   891	          style={[
   892	            styles.carouselInner,
   893	            {
   894	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselInnerTranslateY }],
   895	            },
   896	          ]}
   897	        >
   898	          <Pressable
   899	            onPress={goToPreviousPage}
   900	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   901	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   902	          >
   903	            <Animated.View
   904	              style={[
   905	                styles.arrowBox,
   906	                {
   907	                  opacity: leftArrowOpacity,
   908	                  transform: [
   909	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   910	                  ],
   911	                },
   912	              ]}
   913	            >
   914	              {renderArrowChevron("left")}
   915	            </Animated.View>
   916	          </Pressable>
   917	
   918	          <Pressable
   919	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   920	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   921	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   922	            style={styles.carouselActiveWrap}
   923	          >
   924	            <Text
   925	              numberOfLines={1}
   926	              adjustsFontSizeToFit
   927	              minimumFontScale={0.88}
   928	              style={[
   929	                styles.carouselActiveText,
   930	                styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   931	                {
   932	                  transform: [
   933	                    { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   934	                  ],
   935	                },
   936	              ]}
   937	            >
   938	              {activeLink}
   939	            </Text>
   940	          </Pressable>
   941	
   942	          <Pressable
   943	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   944	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   945	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   946	          >
   947	            <Animated.View
   948	              style={[
   949	                styles.arrowBox,
   950	                {
   951	                  opacity: rightArrowOpacity,
   952	                  transform: [
   953	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   954	                  ],
   955	                },
   956	              ]}
   957	            >
   958	              {renderArrowChevron("right")}
   959	            </Animated.View>
   960	          </Pressable>
   961	        </Animated.View>
   962	
   963	        <Animated.View
   964	          pointerEvents="none"
   965	          style={[
   966	            styles.carouselIndicatorSeparator,
   967	            {
   968	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   969	            },
   970	          ]}
   971	        />
   972	
   973	        <Animated.View
   974	          pointerEvents="none"
   975	          style={[
   976	            styles.carouselIndicatorBar,
   977	            {
   978	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   979	            },
   980	          ]}
   981	        >
   982	          <View style={styles.carouselIndicatorTrack}>
   983	            {navPages.map((pageName) => (
   984	              <View
   985	                key={`carousel-indicator-track-${pageName}`}
   986	                style={styles.carouselIndicatorSegment}
   987	              />
   988	            ))}
   989	          </View>
   990	
   991	          <Animated.View
   992	            style={[
   993	              styles.carouselIndicatorActiveSegment,
   994	              {
   995	                width: indicatorSegmentWidth,
   996	                transform: [{ translateX: indicatorTranslateX }],
   997	              },
   998	            ]}
   999	          />
  1000	        </Animated.View>
  1001	      </Animated.View>
  1002	
  1003	    </View>
  1004	  );
  1005	
  1006	  const centerShadow = (
  1007	    <Animated.View
  1008	      pointerEvents="none"
  1009	      style={[
  1010	        styles.headerCenterShadow,
  1011	        {
  1012	          opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
  1013	          transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
  1014	        },
  1015	      ]}
  1016	    >
  1017	      {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
  1018	        <>
  1019	          {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
  1020	            <View
  1021	              key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
  1022	              style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
  1023	            />
  1024	          ))}
  1025	        </>
  1026	      )}
  1027	    </Animated.View>
  1028	  );
  1029	
  1030	  const hero = (
  1031	    <View
  1032	      style={styles.hero}
  1033	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
  1034	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
  1035	    >
  1036	      <Animated.Image
  1037	        source={heroImageSource}
  1038	        style={[
  1039	          isMainHeroPage ? styles.heroImageWidthFit : styles.heroImage,
  1040	          heroImageFrameStyle,
  1041	          heroImageTransformStyle,
  1042	          heroImageOpacityStyle,
  1043	        ]}
  1044	        resizeMode={isMainHeroPage ? "contain" : "cover"}
  1045	      />
  1046	      {isMainHeroPage
  1047	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1048	            "cheeseboard-current",
  1049	            cheeseboardBaseTranslateX,
  1050	            cheeseboardBaseOpacity
  1051	          )
  1052	        : null}
  1053	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1054	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1055	            "cheeseboard-outgoing",
  1056	            cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX
  1057	          )
  1058	        : null}
  1059	      {shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews ? (
  1060	        <>
  1061	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1062	            "cheeseboard-previous-preview",
  1063	            cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX
  1064	          )}
  1065	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1066	            "cheeseboard-next-preview",
  1067	            cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX
  1068	          )}
  1069	        </>
  1070	      ) : null}
  1071	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1072	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1073	            "cheeseboard-incoming",
  1074	            cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX,
  1075	            cheeseboardIncomingOpacity
  1076	          )
  1077	        : null}
  1078	      {!isMainHeroPage ? (
  1079	        <Animated.View
  1080	          pointerEvents="none"
  1081	          style={[
  1082	            styles.heroVerticalFadePanel,
  1083	            {
  1084	              transform: [{ translateY: heroVerticalFadeTranslateY }],
  1085	            },
  1086	          ]}
  1087	        >
  1088	          <LinearGradient
  1089	            colors={["rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)", heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor]}
  1090	            locations={[0, 1]}
  1091	            style={styles.heroVerticalFadeFeather}
  1092	          />
  1093	          <View
  1094	            style={[
  1095	              styles.heroVerticalFadeSolid,
  1096	              { backgroundColor: heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor },
  1097	            ]}
  1098	          />
  1099	        </Animated.View>
  1100	      ) : null}
  1101	    </View>
  1102	  );
  1103	
  1104	  const heroOnlyLayer = (
  1105	    <Animated.View
  1106	      style={[
  1107	        styles.header,
  1108	        {
  1109	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1110	        },
  1111	      ]}
  1112	    >
  1113	      {centerShadow}
  1114	      <View style={styles.heroOnlySpacer} />
  1115	      {Platform.OS === "ios" ? (
  1116	        <Animated.View
  1117	          style={{
  1118	            transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
  1119	          }}
  1120	        >
  1121	          {hero}
  1122	        </Animated.View>
  1123	      ) : (
  1124	        hero
  1125	      )}
  1126	    </Animated.View>
  1127	  );
  1128	
  1129	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
  1130	  if (showOnlyHero) return heroOnlyLayer;
  1131	
  1132	  return (
  1133	    <Animated.View
  1134	      style={[
  1135	        styles.header,
  1136	        {
  1137	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1138	        },
  1139	      ]}
  1140	    >
  1141	      <Animated.View style={styles.orangeBar}>
  1142	        <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable} onPress={() => goToPage("home")}>
  1143	          <Text
  1144	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
  1145	            minimumFontScale={0.78}
  1146	            numberOfLines={1}
  1147	            style={styles.logoText}
  1148	          >
  1149	            Alla Vostra
  1150	          </Text>
  1151	        </Pressable>
  1152	
  1153	        {dimHeaderExceptShopButton ? (
  1154	          <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarDimOverlay} />
  1155	        ) : null}
  1156	
  1157	        <Pressable
  1158	          style={[
  1159	            styles.shopButtonWrap,
  1160	            dimHeaderExceptShopButton && styles.shopButtonWrapSpotlight,
  1161	          ]}
  1162	          onPress={() => goToPage("shop")}
  1163	        >
  1164	          <ButtonShadowPlate style={styles.shopButtonShadowPlate} />
  1165	          <View style={styles.shopButton}>
  1166	            <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
  1167	          </View>
  1168	        </Pressable>
  1169	
  1170	        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarBottomHairline} />
  1171	      </Animated.View>
  1172	
  1173	      {showCarousel && showHero && centerShadow}
  1174	      {showCarousel && carousel}
  1175	      {showHero && hero}
  1176	    </Animated.View>
  1177	  );
  1178	}


============================================================
FILE: mob/components/PageDivider.js
============================================================

     1	import { Animated } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     4	
     5	const topDividerFadeScrollDistance = 240;
     6	
     7	export default function PageDivider({
     8	  expandedSpacing = false,
     9	  fadeScrollDistance = topDividerFadeScrollDistance,
    10	  fadeWithScrollY = null,
    11	}) {
    12	  const fadeOpacity = fadeWithScrollY?.interpolate({
    13	    inputRange: [0, fadeScrollDistance],
    14	    outputRange: [1, 0],
    15	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    16	  });
    17	
    18	  return (
    19	    <Animated.View
    20	      style={[
    21	        sharedStyles.pageDivider,
    22	        expandedSpacing && sharedStyles.expandedPageDivider,
    23	        fadeOpacity && { opacity: fadeOpacity },
    24	      ]}
    25	    />
    26	  );
    27	}

     1	import { Image, Text, View } from "react-native";

    36	}

FILE: mob/components/ScreenFade.js

     1	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
     2	import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	export default function ScreenFade({ showTopFade = true, topOffset = 84 }) {
     5	  return (
     6	    <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.overlay}>
     7	      {showTopFade ? (
     8	        <LinearGradient
     9	          pointerEvents="none"
    10	          colors={[
    11	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    12	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    13	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    14	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    15	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    16	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    17	          ]}
    18	          locations={[0, 0.14, 0.32, 0.52, 0.72, 1]}
    19	          style={[styles.topFade, { top: topOffset }]}
    20	        />
    21	      ) : null}
    22	
    23	      <LinearGradient
    24	        pointerEvents="none"
    25	        colors={[
    26	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    27	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    28	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    29	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    30	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    31	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    32	        ]}
    33	        locations={[0, 0.28, 0.48, 0.68, 0.86, 1]}
    34	        style={styles.bottomFade}
    35	      />
    36	    </View>
    37	  );
    38	}
    39	
    40	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    41	  overlay: {
    42	    position: "absolute",
    43	    top: 0,
    44	    left: 0,
    45	    right: 0,
    46	    bottom: 0,
    47	    zIndex: 500000,
    48	    elevation: 500000,
    49	  },
    50	
    51	  topFade: {
    52	    position: "absolute",
    53	    left: 0,
    54	    right: 0,
    55	    height: 260,
    56	  },
    57	
    58	  bottomFade: {
    59	    position: "absolute",
    60	    left: 0,
    61	    right: 0,
    62	    bottom: 0,
    63	    height: 150,
    64	  },
    65	});

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}
